Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any bonding module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 173. The projective resolution has length at most 725 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to bonding theory, we decompose the problem into 173 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 725. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
